# NYC What To Do

**Rolling window:** 2026-11-17 through 2026-11-23  
**Updated:** 2026-11-17T11:05:07+00:00  
**Book-ahead watch:** through 2027-03-17

## Best date plans

### 2026-11-21 — Lakeside Open-Air Ice Rink — Opening Day + Lightscape Lodge at Yellow Magnolia Café

**Lakeside Open-Air Ice Rink — Opening Day, then a meal.** Seasonal American at Lightscape Lodge at Yellow Magnolia Café. It is a 17-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 9 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=171+East+Drive%2C+Prospect+Park%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11226&destination=Brooklyn+Botanic+Garden%2C+990+Washington+Avenue+entrance%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11225&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://lakeside.prospectpark.org/activity/public-ice-skate/) · [Event booking](https://apps.daysmartrecreation.com/dash/x/lakeside/programs/24/level?facility_ids=1&season_id=69) · [Restaurant](https://www.bbg.org/feature/lightscape/lightscape_lodge_dinners) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/yellow-magnolia-cafe)

### 2026-11-23 — Lakeside Monday-Special Ice Skating + Lightscape Lodge at Yellow Magnolia Café

**Lakeside Monday-Special Ice Skating, then a meal.** Seasonal American at Lightscape Lodge at Yellow Magnolia Café. It is a 17-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 9 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=171+East+Drive%2C+Prospect+Park%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11226&destination=Brooklyn+Botanic+Garden%2C+990+Washington+Avenue+entrance%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11225&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://lakeside.prospectpark.org/activity/public-ice-skate/) · [Event booking](https://apps.daysmartrecreation.com/dash/x/lakeside/programs/24/level?facility_ids=1&season_id=69) · [Restaurant](https://www.bbg.org/feature/lightscape/lightscape_lodge_dinners) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/yellow-magnolia-cafe)

### 2026-11-20 — Brasserie Boulud + The Danish Girl in Concert

**Dinner, then The Danish Girl in Concert.** French at Brasserie Boulud. It is a 1-minute walk from the venue. The Danish Girl in Concert starts at 7:30 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=1900+Broadway%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10023&destination=1887+Broadway%2C+New+York%2C+NY&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.lincolncenter.org/series/lincoln-center-presents/the-danish-girl-in-concert) · [Event booking](https://tickets.lincolncenter.org/en/booking/production/33631) · [Restaurant](https://www.brasserieboulud.com/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/brasserie-boulud-nyc)

### 2026-11-21 — Barnard/Columbia Dances at New York Live Arts — Saturday Matinee + Café Mogador — East Village

**Barnard/Columbia Dances at New York Live Arts — Saturday Matinee, then a meal.** Moroccan and Middle Eastern at Café Mogador — East Village. It is 1.2 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 2 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=219+West+19th+Street%2C+New+York%2C+NY&destination=101+Saint+Marks+Place%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10009&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://newyorklivearts.org/event/barnard-columbia-dances-at-new-york-live-arts-5/) · [Event booking](https://newyorklivearts.my.salesforce-sites.com/ticket/#/events/a0SVt00000gDW57MAG) · [Restaurant](https://www.cafemogador.com/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/cafe-mogador-east-village)

### 2026-11-21 — Spoons Toons & Booze + Altbau

**Spoons Toons & Booze, then a meal.** Bakery and café at Altbau. It is a 7-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 11:15 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=136+Metropolitan+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11249&destination=184+North+8th+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11211&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://nitehawkcinema.com/williamsburg/special_events/stb-november-2026/) · [Event booking](https://nitehawkcinema.com/williamsburg/purchase/24721307/) · [Restaurant](https://altbaubakery.com/)

### 2026-11-21 — Pioneer Works Fall Saturdays — extended gallery hours + Val's

**Pioneer Works Fall Saturdays — extended gallery hours, then a meal.** American diner and bar food at Val's. It is a 3-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 12 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=159+Pioneer+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11231&destination=347+Van+Brunt+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11231&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://pioneerworks.org/programs/fall-saturdays4) · [Restaurant](https://www.instagram.com/valsredhook/)

### 2026-11-21 — Ready for Occupancy! Early Apartment Houses in Greenwich Village + Altbau

**Ready for Occupancy! Early Apartment Houses in Greenwich Village, then a meal.** Bakery and café at Altbau. It is 2.5 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Greenwich+Village+walking+tour&destination=184+North+8th+Street%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11211&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.mas.org/events/ready-for-occupancy/) · [Event booking](https://secure.mas.org/forms/ready-for-occupancy-early-apartment-houses-in-greenwich-village-registration) · [Restaurant](https://altbaubakery.com/)

### 2026-11-23 — NYC Resistor Public Craft Night + Café Paulette

**NYC Resistor Public Craft Night, then dinner.** French bistro at Café Paulette. It is a 14-minute walk from the venue. It runs to 9:30 pm, so this is a late stop afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=87+3rd+Ave%2C+4th+Floor%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&destination=1+South+Elliott+Place%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.nycresistor.com/) · [Event booking](https://www.eventbrite.com/e/public-craft-night-mondays-and-thursdays-tickets-162729866411) · [Restaurant](https://www.cafepaulette.com/) · [Reservations](https://resy.com/cities/new-york-ny/venues/cafe-paulette)

### 2026-11-19

#### [Winter Lantern Festival — Opening Night](https://winterlanternfestival.com/queens/)

5:00 PM · outdoors · Advertised tickets from $18; selected-session price may differ · Queens County Farm Museum · Floral Park · Queens · Score 65.0  
An outdoor evening trail of handcrafted illuminated Chinese lanterns across Queens Farm, with food vendors and additional entertainment.
**Timing:** Operating window 5–10pm; last entry 9pm. Choose a timed ticket; typical visit is approximately one hour.
**Practical note:** Runs rain or shine. Dress for an outdoor evening and allow extra travel time to eastern Queens.

#### [Holiday Train Show Sensory Friendly Morning](https://www.nybg.org/event/holiday-train-show-2026/holiday-train-show-sensory-friendly-mornings/)

9:00 AM · outdoors · Special-session ticket required; price not exposed in the public page · New York Botanical Garden, Haupt Conservatory · Bronx Park · Bronx · Score 54.0  
A quieter garden-program session around model trains and New York landmarks made from plant materials. Videos and music are turned off; trains and waterfalls remain running. Open to adults and families who prefer a calmer visit.
**Timing:** Special quieter viewing session 9–11am; ticket also includes outdoor garden access.
**Practical note:** The special session is indoors, with outdoor gardens included afterward. A quiet space and alternate exit are provided. Allow extra time for the Botanical Garden station's pedestrian detour.
**Why now:** November 21 is one of only two sensory-friendly mornings announced for this train-show season.

#### [Lakeside Monday-Special Ice Skating](https://lakeside.prospectpark.org/activity/public-ice-skate/)

9:00 AM · sports · $5 cash / $5.15 card admission; skate rental additional · LeFrak Center at Lakeside · Prospect Park · Brooklyn · Score 68.0  
Public ice skating beside Prospect Park's lake with the operator's discounted Monday admission, available after the open-air rink's November 21 opening.
**Timing:** Open-air rink 9am–6pm; covered rink public skating noon–4:45pm. These are operating windows, not a single continuous booked session.
**Practical note:** Admission is $5 cash or $5.15 by card; rentals add $10 cash or $10.29 by card. November 23 is not one of the published Monday-special exclusions.
**Why now:** The first Monday after the open-air rink opens, with a verified discounted rate.
[Official](https://lakeside.prospectpark.org/activity/public-ice-skate/) · [Tickets / booking](https://apps.daysmartrecreation.com/dash/x/lakeside/programs/24/level?facility_ids=1&season_id=69)

#### [Cinématon, Program 1](https://www.anthologyfilmarchives.org/film_screenings/calendar?view=list&month=11&year=2026#showing-62302)

## Where to eat

NYC dining picks for November 17, 2026: happy hours, seasonal menus, Thanksgiving meals and recent openings, with closures and uncertain terms flagged.

Dining research: 2026-11-17T10:41:11+00:00

No current Michelin stars or Guide selections were independently verified in this review, and none are claimed. Michelin sources were not checked; historical awards and chefs' credentials do not establish current restaurant stars.

### Mermaid Oyster Bar: happy-hour shellfish and late-night oysters

Deals & happy hours — Mermaid Oyster Bar Times Square

The official menu lists $2 oysters and clams, small seafood snacks and discounted drinks. A separate nightly bar offer lists $1.50 oysters.

Price: Happy hour: chef's-choice oysters or littleneck clams $2 each, minimum six; mini fish taco $4; mini chowder $4; selected cocktails and wines $12; beers $9. Late bar oysters $1.50 each.  
Schedule: Menu: Monday 3:30pm–close; Tuesday–Sunday 3:30–6:30pm. Separate oyster offer nightly 7pm–close, bar only.  
Conditions: Six-piece minimum applies to the $2 shellfish offer; the late-offer minimum is unstated. Location page says happy hour daily 3:30–6:30pm, so confirm Monday's extended window. Beverage minimums, tax, tip and service charges are unstated. Holiday exceptions unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://themermaidnyc.getbento.com/the-mermaid-inn-times-square-menus/)

### Charlie Bird's nightly Early Bird bar menu

Deals & happy hours — Charlie Bird

An early-evening bar option with $10 drink specials, $2 oysters and discounted food.

Price: Drink specials $10; oysters $2 each; Tuscan fried chicken $12; fries $10; farro salad $16; crudo tasting $25 per person; gnocchi rosa $22; cacio e pepe $23.  
Schedule: Every night 5–6pm at the bar.  
Conditions: Menu also advertises Wednesday–Sunday 3–5pm, but the location page confirms only 5–6pm and lists dinner opening at 5pm; the earlier window needs confirmation. Minimum purchases, reservation requirements, tax, tip and service charges unstated. Holiday exceptions unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://www.charliebirdnyc.com/menus/)

### Vin Sur Vingt Midtown East: $40 early prix fixe

Deals & happy hours — Vin Sur Vingt Bistro

An early French dinner with appetizer and main-course choices, plus separate early-glass and late-bottle wine promotions.

Price: Prix fixe $40; dessert +$10. Selected wine glasses $10 before 6pm. Any wine bottle 25% off from 10pm.  
Schedule: Prix fixe 4–7pm; selected wine glasses opening–6pm; bottle discount 10pm–close. Opens daily at 4pm; closes midnight Sunday–Wednesday and 1am Thursday–Saturday.  
Conditions: The HTML lists onion soup or country pâté, followed by mussels and fries or salmon; its $10 dessert options are crème brûlée or profiteroles. The linked PDF additionally lists endive salad and a burger, and substitutes a seasonal fruit tart for profiteroles. Confirm available choices; neither version establishes purchase minimums or holiday exceptions. Tax, gratuity and service charges unstated.  
[Source](https://www.vsvwinebars.com/location/vin-sur-vingt-wine-bar-bistro-e-51st-st/)

### Le Gratin's weekday set lunch and bar-only frites specials

Deals & happy hours — Le Gratin

Two useful ways to eat at Daniel Boulud's bistro: a weekday prix-fixe lunch or a separate early-evening bar menu.

Price: Lunch: two courses $45 per person or three $55. Lunch supplements: beef tartare +$5, cheeseburger +$5, steak frites +$8. Happy hour: selected frites entrées $30; mini martinis $8 separately.  
Schedule: Lunch Monday–Friday 11:30am–2:30pm. Happy hour Tuesday–Friday 5–7pm at the bar.  
Conditions: Happy-hour choices are steak, mussels, chicken or steak tartare with fries; walk-ins welcome. The $30 entrée does not include the $8 martini. Additional purchase requirements, tax, tip and service charges unstated. Holiday exceptions unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://legratinnyc.com/our-menu/lunch)

### Junoon's $43 two-course or $49 three-course lunch

Deals & happy hours — Junoon

The lunch Prestige menu includes basmati rice and naan, with vegetarian, seafood and meat choices.

Price: Two-course Prestige lunch $43; three courses $49; basmati rice and naan included.  
Schedule: Official location page lists lunch Friday–Sunday 11:45am–2:30pm; no Tuesday lunch listed.  
Conditions: Menu states a 20% service charge for parties of five or more. Location page welcomes children above age 10 and specifies smart casual dress, excluding gym shorts, tank tops and flip-flops. Reservations recommended. Tax and additional gratuity treatment unstated; holiday exceptions unconfirmed.  
[Source](https://www.junoonnyc.com/menus/)

### Frenzie's late-night pizza-and-drink bundles

Deals & happy hours — Frenzie

A late-night alternative to ordering pizza and cocktails separately, from the team behind neighboring Confidant.

Price: Cheese pie with a pitcher of Narragansett $40; cheese pie with two daiquiris $45; cheese pie with two martinis $50. Pepperoni +$4.  
Schedule: Late-night menu after 10pm Thursday–Saturday; location page lists kitchen closing at 1:30am on those nights.  
Conditions: Walk-in only. Bundles include the specified drinks; substitutions unstated. Menu subject to seasonal availability. Tax, tip, service charges and holiday exceptions unknown. Not offered on Tuesday's regular dinner schedule.  
[Source](https://www.frenzienyc.com/menus/)

### Bronx Alehouse includes a drink with weekend brunch

Deals & happy hours — Bronx Alehouse

Every brunch item includes a brunch cocktail or draft beer, making this a concrete Bronx value option rather than an unpriced happy-hour lead.

Price: One brunch cocktail or draft beer free with every brunch item; food prices unknown from the readable menu.  
Schedule: Saturday and Sunday 11am–3pm.  
Conditions: Requires purchase of a brunch item; not bottomless. Eligible cocktail and beer selections unstated. Brunch menu specifies 20% gratuity for parties of six or more. Tax, reservation requirements and holiday exceptions unknown.  
[Source](https://www.bronxalehouse.com/)

Price: $72.

### Jingle Jangle holiday takeover at The Dead Rabbit

Pop-ups — The Dead Rabbit

A finite holiday takeover across all three floors, with seasonal food, themed cocktails and decorations. Closed Christmas Day.

Schedule: November 16, 2026–January 3, 2027, except December 25. Sunday–Thursday 11am–2am; Friday–Saturday 11am–3am. Full food menu until 11:45pm; late-night food until 1am Sunday–Thursday and 2am Friday–Saturday.  
Conditions: Walk-ins only after 6pm; reservations open up to two weeks ahead. Walk-in tables have 90 minutes and reservations two hours; bar seating is first come, first served. Under-21s permitted only before 6pm with a parent or guardian at a table. Gratuity added for parties of five or more or checks over $200; rate unstated. Closed December 25. ### More restaurants

### [Lightscape Lodge at Yellow Magnolia Café](https://www.bbg.org/feature/lightscape/lightscape_lodge_dinners)

Seasonal American · $$$ · Prospect Park · Brooklyn · Score 64.0  
Seasonal seated dinners begin November 20 during Lightscape. The $75 prix fixe includes a shared welcome course, appetizer, entrée and petits fours, excluding drinks, tax and gratuity. Separate Lightscape admission is mandatory; book garden entry at least 45 minutes before dinner.

## Book ahead

#### [Elektra: newly extended final week](https://tfana.org/events/elektra-2026-11-17-730-pm)

2026-12-08 · 7:30 PM · theater · Regular seats $70–$98 plus $7 handling; premium seats $125 plus $7. · Polonsky Shakespeare Center · Fort Greene · Brooklyn · Score 69.0  
Daniel Fish directs Sophokles's tragedy in Anne Carson's translation, with choral music by Ted Hearne. TFANA has added a final week of performances.
**Timing:** The newly extended performances run December 8–13; the first is December 8 at 7:30pm and the last is December 13 at 7:30pm.
**Practical note:** Approximately 80 minutes without intermission. No late seating. Regular tickets remain within the stated $120 budget; premium tickets exceed it.
**Why now:** An officially identified new block offers alternatives to multiple sold-out performances before the revised closing date.
**Hot booking alert:** The official calendar labels December 8–13 newly extended, while November 24 and December 1–3 are sold out and several December 4–6 performances have limited to no availability. The announced run ends December 13.
[Official](https://tfana.org/events/elektra-2026-11-17-730-pm) · [Tickets / booking](https://tfana.org/events/elektra-2026-12-08-730-pm/seats)
**Sale/deadline:** 2026-12-13T19:30:00-05:00

#### [La Distance: final BAM performances](https://www.bam.org/theater/2026/la-distance)

2026-11-29 · 3:00 PM · theater · From $25 before fees; add $11 per ticket online or by phone, or $3 at the box office. · BAM Harvey Theater · Fort Greene · Brooklyn · Score 64.0  
Tiago Rodrigues's North American premiere follows a daughter who leaves Earth for Mars and the father she leaves behind. Alison Dechamps and Adama Diop perform on a rotating stage.
**Timing:** Final performance November 29 at 3pm. Other final-week performances run November 24–28.
**Practical note:** Performed in French with English supertitles; approximately 85 minutes without intermission. Advertised starting prices do not establish availability for the final performance.
**Why now:** The North American premiere finishes in twelve days; this is a closing reminder, not a new ticket-release alert.
**Book soon:** BAM confirms the engagement ends November 29 and lists the final performance at 3pm; there are only six scheduled performances after November 23.
[Official](https://www.bam.org/theater/2026/la-distance) · [Tickets / booking](https://commerce.bam.org/booking/production/56005)
**Sale/deadline:** 2026-11-29T15:00:00-05:00

#### [Two Strangers (Carry a Cake Across New York): Broadway closing](https://twostrangersmusical.com/)

2026-11-29 · 3:00 PM · theater · Longacre Theatre · Theater District · Manhattan · Score 63.0  
Sam Tutty and Christiani Pitts star in an original musical comedy about two strangers navigating New York, family secrets, and unexpected connections.
**Timing:** Final performance November 29 at 3pm; earlier performances are also scheduled during the final week.
**Practical note:** Running time is approximately 2 hours 15 minutes, including an intermission. Current ticket prices and seat availability were not verified.
**Why now:** The announced Broadway closing is only twelve days away, with the final week falling beyond the seven-day discovery window.
**Book soon:** The official production website explicitly announces that the Broadway engagement must close November 29; Playbill confirms the final performance time.
[Official](https://twostrangersmusical.com/) · [Tickets / booking](https://www.telecharge.com/Two-Strangers-Carry-a-Cake-Across-New-York-tickets) · [Discovered via Playbill Productions](https://playbill.com/production/two-strangers-carry-a-cake-across-new-york-broadway-longacre-theatre-2025)
**Sale/deadline:** 2026-11-29T15:00:00-05:00

#### [Carmen de Lavallade: A Life in Motion — January ticket-release watch](https://www.lincolncenter.org/series/lincoln-center-presents/carmen-de-lavallade-a-life-in-motion-248)

2027-03-12 · 7:30 PM · dance · Choose-What-You-Pay; ticket-value options not yet verified. · Alice Tully Hall · Lincoln Square · Manhattan · Score 65.0  
A film-and-performance tribute to Carmen de Lavallade, featuring dancers from Alvin Ailey American Dance Theater and Dance Theatre of Harlem.
**Timing:** Performance March 12 at 7:30pm; approximately one hour. Public ticket release January 21 at noon.
**Practical note:** Choose-What-You-Pay ticketing. January 13 member presale is separate from the January 21 public release; this is a calendar watch, not a recommendation to purchase now.
**Why now:** The public release date is already announced and can be calendared without inventing scarcity or confusing it with the event date.
**On-sale watch:** The event-specific official page explicitly announces general on-sale January 21 at noon, ahead of the March 12 performance.
[Official](https://www.lincolncenter.org/series/lincoln-center-presents/carmen-de-lavallade-a-life-in-motion-248) · [Tickets / booking](https://www.lincolncenter.org/series/lincoln-center-presents/carmen-de-lavallade-a-life-in-motion-248)
**Sale/deadline:** 2027-01-21T12:00:00-05:00

#### [SIX: final Broadway performance](https://sixonbroadway.com/)

2027-01-03 · 7:00 PM · theater · Lena Horne Theatre · Theater District · Manhattan · Score 53.0  
The six wives of Henry VIII reclaim their stories through a concert-style pop musical. The production has announced its final Broadway performance.
**Timing:** The official calendar lists January 3 performances at 2pm and 7pm; 7pm is the last scheduled performance.
**Practical note:** Ordinary ticket prices and remaining seats were not verified. The published $35 student rush requires valid student identification and is not an advance-booking price.
**Why now:** A verified Broadway closing creates a firm planning deadline; no claim of scarce seats is being made.
**On-sale watch:** The official production homepage announces Broadway's final performance on January 3, 2027, and its ticket calendar ends with the 7pm show that day.
[Official](https://sixonbroadway.com/) · [Tickets / booking](https://tickets.broadwaydirect.com/tickets/series/719897/2211791) · [Discovered via Playbill Productions](https://playbill.com/shows/broadway)
